{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016QxyxVoqpwTdhhGYCEgipL","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# FortiLedger360 – Architecture Overview\n     2→Enterprise-grade, layered security platform built on an Event-Driven, Service-Mesh core.  \n     3→All examples below are **real**, compilable C++17 code lifted directly from the production reference implementation (namespaces abridged for clarity).\n     4→\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→## ☰ Logical Layers\n     8→\n     9→```mermaid\n    10→flowchart TD\n    11→    UI[Presentation<br/>& API]\n    12→    ORCH[Orchestration<br/>(SAGA / CQRS)]\n    13→    DOMAIN[Domain<br/>(Pure C++)]\n    14→    INFRA[Infrastructure<br/>(gRPC / DB / FS)]\n    15→    PLATFORM[Platform<br/>(K8s / Istio / Vault)]\n    16→\n    17→    UI-->ORCH-->DOMAIN-->INFRA-->PLATFORM\n    18→```\n    19→\n    20→---\n    21→\n    22→## 1.  Event Bus & Command Pattern  \n    23→\n    24→A thin, header-only message bus guarantees _exactly-once_ local dispatch and acts as the backbone for all commands (`SecurityCommand` derivatives).  \n    25→\n    26→```cpp\n    27→// include/fl360/core/EventBus.hpp\n    28→#pragma once\n    29→#include <functional>\n    30→#include <unordered_map>\n    31→#include <mutex>\n    32→#include <vector>\n    33→#include <typeindex>\n    34→#include <memory>\n    35→\n    36→namespace fl360::core {\n    37→\n    38→class EventBase\n    39→{\n    40→public:\n    41→    virtual ~EventBase() = default;\n    42→};\n    43→\n    44→class HandlerBase\n    45→{\n    46→public:\n    47→    virtual ~HandlerBase() = default;\n    48→    virtual void handle(const EventBase&) = 0;\n    49→};\n    50→\n    51→template <typename E, typename F>\n    52→class Handler final : public HandlerBase\n    53→{\n    54→public:\n    55→    explicit Handler(F&& f) : fn_(std::forward<F>(f)) {}\n    56→    void handle(const EventBase& e) override { fn_(static_cast<const E&>(e)); }\n    57→private:\n    58→    F fn_;\n    59→};\n    60→\n    61→class EventBus\n    62→{\n    63→public:\n    64→    template <typename E, typename F>\n    65→    void subscribe(F&& fn)\n    66→    {\n    67→        const std::type_index key = typeid(E);\n    68→        std::unique_ptr<HandlerBase> h =\n    69→            std::make_unique<Handler<E, F>>(std::forward<F>(fn));\n    70→\n    71→        std::scoped_lock lk(mx_);\n    72→        handlers_[key].emplace_back(std::move(h));\n    73→    }\n    74→\n    75→    template <typename E>\n    76→    void publish(const E& evt) const\n    77→    {\n    78→        const std::type_index key = typeid(E);\n    79→        std::scoped_lock lk(mx_);\n    80→        auto it = handlers_.find(key);\n    81→        if (it == handlers_.end()) return;\n    82→\n    83→        for (const auto& h : it->second)\n    84→            h->handle(evt);\n    85→    }\n    86→\n    87→private:\n    88→    mutable std::mutex mx_;\n    89→    std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::vector<std::unique_ptr<HandlerBase>>> handlers_;\n    90→};\n    91→\n    92→} // namespace fl360::core\n    93→```\n    94→\n    95→### Command Abstractions\n    96→\n    97→```cpp\n    98→// include/fl360/domain/commands/SecurityCommand.hpp\n    99→#pragma once\n   100→#include <string>\n   101→#include \"EventBus.hpp\"\n   102→\n   103→namespace fl360::domain {\n   104→\n   105→struct SecurityCommand : public core::EventBase\n   106→{\n   107→    std::string tenantId;\n   108→    virtual std::string name() const = 0;\n   109→};\n   110→\n   111→struct InitiateSecurityScan final : public SecurityCommand\n   112→{\n   113→    enum class ScanDepth { kLight, kDeep } depth = ScanDepth::kLight;\n   114→    std::string name() const override { return \"InitiateSecurityScan\"; }\n   115→};\n   116→\n   117→struct RollClusterBackup final : public SecurityCommand\n   118→{\n   119→    bool offsite = false;\n   120→    std::string name() const override { return \"RollClusterBackup\"; }\n   121→};\n   122→\n   123→} // namespace fl360::domain\n   124→```\n   125→\n   126→---\n   127→\n   128→## 2.  Strategy Pattern – Pluggable Scan Engines\n   129→\n   130→```cpp\n   131→// include/fl360/domain/scan/IScanStrategy.hpp\n   132→#pragma once\n   133→#include <string>\n   134→namespace fl360::domain {\n   135→\n   136→class IScanStrategy\n   137→{\n   138→public:\n   139→    virtual ~IScanStrategy() = default;\n   140→    virtual void execute(const std::string& tenantId) = 0;\n   141→    virtual const char* id() const = 0;\n   142→};\n   143→\n   144→} // namespace fl360::domain\n   145→```\n   146→\n   147→Two concrete strategies:\n   148→\n   149→```cpp\n   150→// src/scan/ContinuousScanStrategy.cpp\n   151→#include \"IScanStrategy.hpp\"\n   152→#include <chrono>\n   153→#include <thread>\n   154→#include <iostream>\n   155→\n   156→namespace fl360::domain {\n   157→\n   158→class ContinuousScanStrategy : public IScanStrategy\n   159→{\n   160→public:\n   161→    void execute(const std::string& tenantId) override\n   162→    {\n   163→        std::cout << \"[SCAN] Continuous scan for \" << tenantId << '\\n';\n   164→        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds(2)); // simulate\n   165→    }\n   166→    const char* id() const override { return \"continuous\"; }\n   167→};\n   168→\n   169→class PayAsYouGoScanStrategy : public IScanStrategy\n   170→{\n   171→public:\n   172→    void execute(const std::string& tenantId) override\n   173→    {\n   174→        std::cout << \"[SCAN] On-demand deep scan for \" << tenantId << '\\n';\n   175→    }\n   176→    const char* id() const override { return \"payg\"; }\n   177→};\n   178→\n   179→} // namespace fl360::domain\n   180→```\n   181→\n   182→Strategy registry (hot-swappable at runtime):\n   183→\n   184→```cpp\n   185→// include/fl360/domain/scan/ScanRegistry.hpp\n   186→#pragma once\n   187→#include <memory>\n   188→#include <unordered_map>\n   189→#include <stdexcept>\n   190→\n   191→namespace fl360::domain {\n   192→\n   193→class ScanRegistry\n   194→{\n   195→public:\n   196→    template <typename T>\n   197→    void registerStrategy()\n   198→    {\n   199→        static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<IScanStrategy, T>);\n   200→        auto ptr = std::make_unique<T>();\n   201→        strategies_.emplace(ptr->id(), std::move(ptr));\n   202→    }\n   203→\n   204→    IScanStrategy& resolve(const std::string& id) const\n   205→    {\n   206→        auto it = strategies_.find(id);\n   207→        if (it == strategies_.end())\n   208→            throw std::runtime_error(\"Unknown scan strategy: \" + id);\n   209→        return *it->second;\n   210→    }\n   211→\n   212→private:\n   213→    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::unique_ptr<IScanStrategy>> strategies_;\n   214→};\n   215→\n   216→} // namespace fl360::domain\n   217→```\n   218→\n   219→---\n   220→\n   221→## 3.  Chain-of-Responsibility – Compliance Validator Pipeline\n   222→\n   223→```cpp\n   224→// include/fl360/domain/policy/PolicyValidator.hpp\n   225→#pragma once\n   226→#include <memory>\n   227→#include <utility>\n   228→#include <optional>\n   229→\n   230→namespace fl360::domain {\n   231→\n   232→class SecurityContext\n   233→{\n   234→public:\n   235→    bool isCompliant = true; // placeholder\n   236→    std::string reason;\n   237→};\n   238→\n   239→class PolicyValidator\n   240→{\n   241→public:\n   242→    using Ptr = std::unique_ptr<PolicyValidator>;\n   243→    explicit PolicyValidator(Ptr next = nullptr) : next_(std::move(next)) {}\n   244→    virtual ~PolicyValidator() = default;\n   245→\n   246→    void validate(SecurityContext& ctx) const\n   247→    {\n   248→        if (check(ctx) && next_) next_->validate(ctx);\n   249→    }\n   250→\n   251→protected:\n   252→    virtual bool check(SecurityContext& ctx) const = 0;\n   253→\n   254→private:\n   255→    Ptr next_;\n   256→};\n   257→\n   258→// --- concrete rules ----------------------------------\n   259→\n   260→class IsTenantActive : public PolicyValidator\n   261→{\n   262→public:\n   263→    using PolicyValidator::PolicyValidator;\n   264→protected:\n   265→    bool check(SecurityContext& ctx) const override\n   266→    {\n   267→        const bool ok = ctx.isCompliant; // assume call to repo\n   268→        if (!ok) ctx.reason = \"Tenant not active\";\n   269→        return ok;\n   270→    }\n   271→};\n   272→\n   273→class MeetsSLAPolicy : public PolicyValidator\n   274→{\n   275→public:\n   276→    using PolicyValidator::PolicyValidator;\n   277→protected:\n   278→    bool check(SecurityContext& ctx) const override\n   279→    {\n   280→        const bool ok = ctx.isCompliant; // imagine SLA lookup\n   281→        if (!ok) ctx.reason = \"SLA breach detected\";\n   282→        return ok;\n   283→    }\n   284→};\n   285→\n   286→} // namespace fl360::domain\n   287→```\n   288→\n   289→Assembling the chain:\n   290→\n   291→```cpp\n   292→fl360::domain::PolicyValidator::Ptr chain =\n   293→    std::make_unique<IsTenantActive>(\n   294→        std::make_unique<MeetsSLAPolicy>());\n   295→```\n   296→\n   297→---\n   298→\n   299→## 4.  Observer Pattern – Real-Time Metrics Tap\n   300→\n   301→```cpp\n   302→// include/fl360/infra/metrics/EventMetricsSink.hpp\n   303→#pragma once\n   304→#include \"EventBus.hpp\"\n   305→#include <iostream>\n   306→\n   307→namespace fl360::infra::metrics {\n   308→\n   309→class EventMetricsSink\n   310→{\n   311→public:\n   312→    explicit EventMetricsSink(core::EventBus& bus)\n   313→    {\n   314→        bus.subscribe<domain::SecurityCommand>(\n   315→            [this](const domain::SecurityCommand& cmd) {\n   316→                std::cout << \"[METRICS] Command: \" << cmd.name()\n   317→                          << \" tenant=\" << cmd.tenantId << '\\n';\n   318→            });\n   319→    }\n   320→};\n   321→\n   322→} // namespace fl360::infra::metrics\n   323→```\n   324→\n   325→---\n   326→\n   327→## 5.  Service Mesh – gRPC Skeleton (mutual-TLS ready)\n   328→\n   329→```cpp\n   330→// proto/backupnode.proto\n   331→/*\n   332→syntax = \"proto3\";\n   333→package fl360.backupnode;\n   334→service BackupNode {\n   335→  rpc RollClusterBackup(RollClusterBackupRequest) returns (BackupStatus);\n   336→}\n   337→message RollClusterBackupRequest {\n   338→  string tenant_id = 1;\n   339→  bool   offsite   = 2;\n   340→}\n   341→message BackupStatus {\n   342→  bool   ok      = 1;\n   343→  string message = 2;\n   344→}\n   345→*/\n   346→```\n   347→\n   348→```cpp\n   349→// src/backupnode/BackupNodeService.cpp\n   350→#include \"backupnode.grpc.pb.h\"\n   351→#include <grpcpp/grpcpp.h>\n   352→#include <iostream>\n   353→\n   354→namespace fl360::infra {\n   355→\n   356→class BackupNodeService final : public backupnode::BackupNode::Service\n   357→{\n   358→public:\n   359→    grpc::Status RollClusterBackup(\n   360→        grpc::ServerContext* ctx,\n   361→        const backupnode::RollClusterBackupRequest* req,\n   362→        backupnode::BackupStatus* rsp) override\n   363→    {\n   364→        std::cout << \"[BACKUP] rolling backup for \" << req->tenant_id() << '\\n';\n   365→        rsp->set_ok(true);\n   366→        rsp->set_message(\"Backup scheduled\");\n   367→        return grpc::Status::OK;\n   368→    }\n   369→};\n   370→\n   371→} // namespace fl360::infra\n   372→```\n   373→\n   374→---\n   375→\n   376→## 6.  Wiring It Together – `main.cpp`\n   377→\n   378→```cpp\n   379→// src/main.cpp\n   380→#include \"EventBus.hpp\"\n   381→#include \"commands/SecurityCommand.hpp\"\n   382→#include \"scan/ScanRegistry.hpp\"\n   383→#include \"scan/IScanStrategy.hpp\"\n   384→#include \"policy/PolicyValidator.hpp\"\n   385→#include \"metrics/EventMetricsSink.hpp\"\n   386→\n   387→#include <iostream>\n   388→\n   389→using namespace fl360;\n   390→\n   391→int main()\n   392→{\n   393→    core::EventBus bus;\n   394→    infra::metrics::EventMetricsSink metrics{bus};\n   395→\n   396→    // Register strategies\n   397→    domain::ScanRegistry registry;\n   398→    registry.registerStrategy<domain::ContinuousScanStrategy>();\n   399→    registry.registerStrategy<domain::PayAsYouGoScanStrategy>();\n   400→\n   401→    // Build policy chain\n   402→    domain::PolicyValidator::Ptr policies =\n   403→        std::make_unique<domain::IsTenantActive>(\n   404→            std::make_unique<domain::MeetsSLAPolicy>());\n   405→\n   406→    // Subscribe orchestrator to security commands\n   407→    bus.subscribe<domain::InitiateSecurityScan>([&](const auto& cmd) {\n   408→        domain::SecurityContext ctx;\n   409→        policies->validate(ctx);\n   410→        if (!ctx.reason.empty()) {\n   411→            std::cerr << \"[ERROR] \" << ctx.reason << '\\n';\n   412→            return;\n   413→        }\n   414→        auto& engine = registry.resolve(\"continuous\");\n   415→        engine.execute(cmd.tenantId);\n   416→    });\n   417→\n   418→    // Publish a command\n   419→    domain::InitiateSecurityScan scanCmd;\n   420→    scanCmd.tenantId = \"acme-corp\";\n   421→    scanCmd.depth    = domain::InitiateSecurityScan::ScanDepth::kDeep;\n   422→    bus.publish(scanCmd);\n   423→\n   424→    return 0;\n   425→}\n   426→```\n   427→\n   428→Build & Run:\n   429→\n   430→```bash\n   431→g++ -std=c++17 -pthread -Iinclude src/main.cpp -o fl360\n   432→./fl360\n   433→```\n   434→\n   435→Expected output:\n   436→\n   437→```\n   438→[METRICS] Command: InitiateSecurityScan tenant=acme-corp\n   439→[SCAN] Continuous scan for acme-corp\n   440→```\n   441→\n   442→---\n   443→\n   444→## 7.  Key Takeaways\n   445→• Single-header EventBus keeps the Domain pure and testable.  \n   446→• Strategy registry allows per-tenant customization without redeploy.  \n   447→• Policy chain enforces compliance before any privileged action.  \n   448→• Observers stream metrics/logs with zero coupling.  \n   449→• gRPC services participate in Istio service-mesh with mutual-TLS.  \n   450→\n   451→> FortiLedger360 combines proven patterns into a cohesive, maintainable security platform ready for enterprise scale.\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. Event Bus & Command Pattern  \n\nA thin, header-only message bus guarantees _exactly-once_ local dispatch and acts as the backbone for all commands (`SecurityCommand` derivatives).  \n\n```cpp\n// include/fl360/core/EventBus.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <functional>\n#include <unordered_map>\n#include <mutex>\n#include <vector>\n#include <typeindex>\n#include <memory>\n\nnamespace fl360::core {\n\nclass EventBase\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~EventBase() = default;\n};\n\nclass HandlerBase\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~HandlerBase() = default;\n    virtual void handle(const EventBase&) = 0;\n};\n\ntemplate <typename E, typename F>\nclass Handler final : public HandlerBase\n{\npublic:\n    explicit Handler(F&& f) : fn_(std::forward<F>(f)) {}\n    void handle(const EventBase& e) override { fn_(static_cast<const E&>(e)); }\nprivate:\n    F fn_;\n};\n\nclass EventBus\n{\npublic:\n    template <typename E, typename F>\n    void subscribe(F&& fn)\n    {\n        const std::type_index key = typeid(E);\n        std::unique_ptr<HandlerBase> h =\n            std::make_unique<Handler<E, F>>(std::forward<F>(fn));\n\n        std::scoped_lock lk(mx_);\n        handlers_[key].emplace_back(std::move(h));\n    }\n\n    template <typename E>\n    void publish(const E& evt) const\n    {\n        const std::type_index key = typeid(E);\n        std::scoped_lock lk(mx_);\n        auto it = handlers_.find(key);\n        if (it == handlers_.end()) return;\n\n        for (const auto& h : it->second)\n            h->handle(evt);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    mutable std::mutex mx_;\n    std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::vector<std::unique_ptr<HandlerBase>>> handlers_;\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::core\n```\n\n### Command Abstractions\n\n```cpp\n// include/fl360/domain/commands/SecurityCommand.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <string>\n#include \"EventBus.hpp\"\n\nnamespace fl360::domain {\n\nstruct SecurityCommand : public core::EventBase\n{\n    std::string tenantId;\n    virtual std::string name() const = 0;\n};\n\nstruct InitiateSecurityScan final : public SecurityCommand\n{\n    enum class ScanDepth { kLight, kDeep } depth = ScanDepth::kLight;\n    std::string name() const override { return \"InitiateSecurityScan\"; }\n};\n\nstruct RollClusterBackup final : public SecurityCommand\n{\n    bool offsite = false;\n    std::string name() const override { return \"RollClusterBackup\"; }\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::domain\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2.  Strategy Pattern – Pluggable Scan Engines\n\n```cpp\n// include/fl360/domain/scan/IScanStrategy.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <string>\nnamespace fl360::domain {\n\nclass IScanStrategy\n{\npublic:\n    virtual ~IScanStrategy() = default;\n    virtual void execute(const std::string& tenantId) = 0;\n    virtual const char* id() const = 0;\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::domain\n```\n\nTwo concrete strategies:\n\n```cpp\n// src/scan/ContinuousScanStrategy.cpp\n#include \"IScanStrategy.hpp\"\n#include <chrono>\n#include <thread>\n#include <iostream>\n\nnamespace fl360::domain {\n\nclass ContinuousScanStrategy : public IScanStrategy\n{\npublic:\n    void execute(const std::string& tenantId) override\n    {\n        std::cout << \"[SCAN] Continuous scan for \" << tenantId << '\\n';\n        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds(2)); // simulate\n    }\n    const char* id() const override { return \"continuous\"; }\n};\n\nclass PayAsYouGoScanStrategy : public IScanStrategy\n{\npublic:\n    void execute(const std::string& tenantId) override\n    {\n        std::cout << \"[SCAN] On-demand deep scan for \" << tenantId << '\\n';\n    }\n    const char* id() const override { return \"payg\"; }\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::domain\n```\n\nStrategy registry (hot-swappable at runtime):\n\n```cpp\n// include/fl360/domain/scan/ScanRegistry.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <memory>\n#include <unordered_map>\n#include <stdexcept>\n\nnamespace fl360::domain {\n\nclass ScanRegistry\n{\npublic:\n    template <typename T>\n    void registerStrategy()\n    {\n        static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<IScanStrategy, T>);\n        auto ptr = std::make_unique<T>();\n        strategies_.emplace(ptr->id(), std::move(ptr));\n    }\n\n    IScanStrategy& resolve(const std::string& id) const\n    {\n        auto it = strategies_.find(id);\n        if (it == strategies_.end())\n            throw std::runtime_error(\"Unknown scan strategy: \" + id);\n        return *it->second;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::unique_ptr<IScanStrategy>> strategies_;\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::domain\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3.  Chain-of-Responsibility – Compliance Validator Pipeline\n\n```cpp\n// include/fl360/domain/policy/PolicyValidator.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <memory>\n#include <utility>\n#include <optional>\n\nnamespace fl360::domain {\n\nclass SecurityContext\n{\npublic:\n    bool isCompliant = true; // placeholder\n    std::string reason;\n};\n\nclass PolicyValidator\n{\npublic:\n    using Ptr = std::unique_ptr<PolicyValidator>;\n    explicit PolicyValidator(Ptr next = nullptr) : next_(std::move(next)) {}\n    virtual ~PolicyValidator() = default;\n\n    void validate(SecurityContext& ctx) const\n    {\n        if (check(ctx) && next_) next_->validate(ctx);\n    }\n\nprotected:\n    virtual bool check(SecurityContext& ctx) const = 0;\n\nprivate:\n    Ptr next_;\n};\n\n// --- concrete rules ----------------------------------\n\nclass IsTenantActive : public PolicyValidator\n{\npublic:\n    using PolicyValidator::PolicyValidator;\nprotected:\n    bool check(SecurityContext& ctx) const override\n    {\n        const bool ok = ctx.isCompliant; // assume call to repo\n        if (!ok) ctx.reason = \"Tenant not active\";\n        return ok;\n    }\n};\n\nclass MeetsSLAPolicy : public PolicyValidator\n{\npublic:\n    using PolicyValidator::PolicyValidator;\nprotected:\n    bool check(SecurityContext& ctx) const override\n    {\n        const bool ok = ctx.isCompliant; // imagine SLA lookup\n        if (!ok) ctx.reason = \"SLA breach detected\";\n        return ok;\n    }\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::domain\n```\n\nAssembling the chain:\n\n```cpp\nfl360::domain::PolicyValidator::Ptr chain =\n    std::make_unique<IsTenantActive>(\n        std::make_unique<MeetsSLAPolicy>());\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4.  Observer Pattern – Real-Time Metrics Tap\n\n```cpp\n// include/fl360/infra/metrics/EventMetricsSink.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include \"EventBus.hpp\"\n#include <iostream>\n\nnamespace fl360::infra::metrics {\n\nclass EventMetricsSink\n{\npublic:\n    explicit EventMetricsSink(core::EventBus& bus)\n    {\n        bus.subscribe<domain::SecurityCommand>(\n            [this](const domain::SecurityCommand& cmd) {\n                std::cout << \"[METRICS] Command: \" << cmd.name()\n                          << \" tenant=\" << cmd.tenantId << '\\n';\n            });\n    }\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::infra::metrics\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5.  Service Mesh – gRPC Skeleton (mutual-TLS ready)\n\n```cpp\n// proto/backupnode.proto\n/*\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\npackage fl360.backupnode;\nservice BackupNode {\n  rpc RollClusterBackup(RollClusterBackupRequest) returns (BackupStatus);\n}\nmessage RollClusterBackupRequest {\n  string tenant_id = 1;\n  bool   offsite   = 2;\n}\nmessage BackupStatus {\n  bool   ok      = 1;\n  string message = 2;\n}\n*/\n```\n\n```cpp\n// src/backupnode/BackupNodeService.cpp\n#include \"backupnode.grpc.pb.h\"\n#include <grpcpp/grpcpp.h>\n#include <iostream>\n\nnamespace fl360::infra {\n\nclass BackupNodeService final : public backupnode::BackupNode::Service\n{\npublic:\n    grpc::Status RollClusterBackup(\n        grpc::ServerContext* ctx,\n        const backupnode::RollClusterBackupRequest* req,\n        backupnode::BackupStatus* rsp) override\n    {\n        std::cout << \"[BACKUP] rolling backup for \" << req->tenant_id() << '\\n';\n        rsp->set_ok(true);\n        rsp->set_message(\"Backup scheduled\");\n        return grpc::Status::OK;\n    }\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::infra\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6.  Wiring It Together – `main.cpp`\n\n```cpp\n// src/main.cpp\n#include \"EventBus.hpp\"\n#include \"commands/SecurityCommand.hpp\"\n#include \"scan/ScanRegistry.hpp\"\n#include \"scan/IScanStrategy.hpp\"\n#include \"policy/PolicyValidator.hpp\"\n#include \"metrics/EventMetricsSink.hpp\"\n\n#include <iostream>\n\nusing namespace fl360;\n\nint main()\n{\n    core::EventBus bus;\n    infra::metrics::EventMetricsSink metrics{bus};\n\n    // Register strategies\n    domain::ScanRegistry registry;\n    registry.registerStrategy<domain::ContinuousScanStrategy>();\n    registry.registerStrategy<domain::PayAsYouGoScanStrategy>();\n\n    // Build policy chain\n    domain::PolicyValidator::Ptr policies =\n        std::make_unique<domain::IsTenantActive>(\n            std::make_unique<domain::MeetsSLAPolicy>());\n\n    // Subscribe orchestrator to security commands\n    bus.subscribe<domain::InitiateSecurityScan>([&](const auto& cmd) {\n        domain::SecurityContext ctx;\n        policies->validate(ctx);\n        if (!ctx.reason.empty()) {\n            std::cerr << \"[ERROR] \" << ctx.reason << '\\n';\n            return;\n        }\n        auto& engine = registry.resolve(\"continuous\");\n        engine.execute(cmd.tenantId);\n    });\n\n    // Publish a command\n    domain::InitiateSecurityScan scanCmd;\n    scanCmd.tenantId = \"acme-corp\";\n    scanCmd.depth    = domain::InitiateSecurityScan::ScanDepth::kDeep;\n    bus.publish(scanCmd);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\nBuild & Run:\n\n```bash\ng++ -std=c++17 -pthread -Iinclude src/main.cpp -o fl360\n./fl360\n```\n\nExpected output:\n\n```\n[METRICS] Command: InitiateSecurityScan tenant=acme-corp\n[SCAN] Continuous scan for acme-corp\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. High-Level Flow\n    12→\n    13→```mermaid\n    14→sequenceDiagram\n    15→    autonumber\n    16→    actor Client as 🧑‍💻 Tenant / API Consumer\n    17→    participant API as 🌐 Presentation & API\n    18→    participant ORCH as 🔄 Orchestration\n    19→    participant BUS as 📨 Event-Bus\n    20→    participant SCN as 🛡️ ScannerSvc (Domain)\n    21→    participant BCK as 💾 BackupSvc (Domain)\n    22→    participant INF as 🛠️ Platform Adapter\n    23→\n    24→    Client->>API: POST /v1/scan\\n{asset: \"web-1\", tier:\"deep\"}\n    25→    API->>ORCH: Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>\n    26→    ORCH->>BUS: Publish(Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>)\n    27→    %% Broadcast to interested domain services\n    28→    BUS-->>SCN: Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>\n    29→    SCN->>SCN: Validate + Enrich\n    30→    SCN->>BUS: Emit(Evt<SecurityScanStarted>)\n    31→    BUS-->>INF: Evt<SecurityScanStarted>\n    32→    INF->>SCN: gRPC startScan()\n    33→    INF-->>BUS: Evt<SecurityScanProgress>\n    34→    BUS-->>SCN: Evt<SecurityScanProgress>\n    35→    SCN->>BUS: Emit(Evt<SecurityScanCompleted>)\n    36→    BUS-->>ORCH: Evt<SecurityScanCompleted>\n    37→    ORCH->>Client: 200 OK {\"scanId\":\"...\",\"state\":\"COMPLETED\"}\n    38→```\n    39→\n    40→---\n    41→\n    42→## 2. Canonical Event Taxonomy\n    43→\n    44→| Layer              | Prefix | Example                     |\n    45→|--------------------|--------|-----------------------------|\n    46→| Command            | `Cmd`  | `Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>` |\n    47→| Domain Event       | `Evt`  | `Evt<SecurityScanStarted>`  |\n    48→| Integration Event  | `Int`  | `Int<InvoiceGenerated>`     |\n    49→| Query / Response   | `Qry`  | `Qry<TenantUsage>`          |\n    50→\n    51→---\n    52→\n    53→## 3. eventing/InMemoryBus.hpp\n   227→#pragma once\n   228→#include <functional>\n   229→#include <unordered_map>\n   230→#include <vector>\n   231→#include <shared_mutex>\n   232→#include <typeindex>\n   233→#include <any>\n   234→\n   235→namespace fl360::eventing {\n   236→\n   237→class InMemoryBus\n   238→{\n   239→public:\n   240→    template<typename TMsg>\n   241→    using Handler = std::function<void(const TMsg&)>;\n   242→\n   243→    template<typename TMsg>\n   244→    void publish(const TMsg& msg)\n   245→    {\n   246→        std::shared_lock lock(mutex_);\n   247→        auto it = subscribers_.find(std::type_index(typeid(TMsg)));\n   248→        if (it == subscribers_.end()) return;\n   249→\n   250→        for (auto& anyCb : it->second)\n   251→        {\n   252→            auto& cb = *std::any_cast<Handler<TMsg>>(&anyCb);\n   253→            try { cb(msg); } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n   254→                // Never kill the bus thread\n   255→                spdlog::error(\"EventBus handler threw: {}\", ex.what());\n   256→            }\n   257→        }\n   258→    }\n   259→\n   260→    template<typename TMsg>\n   261→    void subscribe(Handler<TMsg> handler)\n   262→    {\n   263→        std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n   264→        subscribers_[std::type_index(typeid(TMsg))].push_back(handler);\n   265→    }\n   266→\n   267→private:\n   268→    std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::vector<std::any>> subscribers_;\n   269→    mutable std::shared_mutex mutex_;\n   270→};\n   271→\n   272→} // namespace fl360::eventing\n   273→```\n   274→\n   275→### 3.6 Domain Service Example – ScannerSvc\n   276→\n   277→```cpp\n   278→// domain/scanner/ScannerService.hpp\n   279→#pragma once\n   280→#include 300→```\n   301→\n   302→```cpp\n   303→// domain/scanner/ScannerService.cpp\n   304→#include \"ScannerService.hpp\"\n   305→#include \"eventing/Envelope.hpp\"\n   306→#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n   307→\n   308→namespace fl360::domain::scanner {\n   309→\n   310→ScannerService::ScannerService(std::shared_ptr<eventing::InMemoryBus> bus)\n   311→    : bus_{std::move(bus)} {}\n   312→\n   313→void ScannerService::handleScan(const api::commands::InitiateSecurityScan& cmd)\n   320→{\n   321→    spdlog::info(\"ScannerSvc received scan request for asset {}\", cmd.assetId);\n   322→\n   323→    // const noexcept override\n   328→        { return \"Evt<SecurityScanStarted>\"; }\n   329→    } evt{.scanId = \"scan-\" + cmd.assetId};\n   330→\n   331→    bus_->publish(evt);\n   332→\n   333→    //   bool        findingsPresent{false};\n   340→        std::string_view name() const noexcept override\n   341→        { return \"Evt<SecurityScanCompleted>\"; }\n   342→    } completed{.scanId = Putting It All Together\n   353→\n   354→```cpp\n   355→// main.cpp\n   356→#include \"eventing/InMemoryBus.hpp\"\n   357→#include \"orchestration/ValidationChain.hpp\"\n   358→#include \"domain/scanner/ScannerService.hpp\"\n   359→#include \"api/commands/InitiateSecurityScan.hpp\"\n   360→#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n   361→\n   362→int main()\n   363→{\n   364→    using namespace fl360;\n   365→\n   366→    auto bus = std::make_shared<eventing::InMemoryBus>();\n   367→\n   368→    // Bootstrap Scanner Domain Service\n   369→    domain::scanner::ScannerService scanner{bus};\n   370→    scanner.start();\n   371→\n   372→    // Build validation chain\n   373→    auto quota   = std::make_shared<orchestration::TenantQuotaValidator>();\n   374→    auto policy  = std::make_shared<orchestration::CompliancePolicyValidator>();\n   375→    quota->linkWith(policy);\n   376→\n   377→    // Example command coming from API layer\n   378→    api::commands::InitiateSecurityScan cmd{\n   379→        .tenantId = \"tenant-42\",\n   380→        .assetId  = \"web-front-1\",\n   381→        .scanTier = \"deep\"\n   382→    };\n   383→\n   384→    if (quota->validate(cmd))\n   385→    {\n   386→        spdlog::info(\"Validation passed – publishing command\");\n   387→        bus->publish(cmd);\n   388→    }\n   389→    else\n   390→    {\n   391→        spdlog::warn(\"Validation failed – rejecting command\");\n   392→    }\n   393→\n   394→    // Block until Ctrl-C (demo only)\n   395→    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds{3});\n   396→}\n   397→```\n   398→\n   399→> The snippet demonstrates a minimal **local** topology.  \n   400→> In production, the same abstractions map to **NATS JetStream** for publication, and gRPC-based **mesh sidecars** replace the in-process bus.\n   401→\n   402→---\n   403→\n   404→## 5. Failure Handling & Observability\n   405→\n   406→1. **Dead-Letter Queue** – When handler exceptions exceed a retry budget, the `Envelope` is routed to `DLQ.<ServiceName>` with exponential back-off metadata.  \n   407→2. **Telemetry** – Each `Envelope` mutates the OpenTelemetry span context (via `correlationId`), enabling distributed tracing across gRPC boundaries.  \n   408→3. **Circuit Breaker** – Outbound adapter calls (e.g., S3 backup storage) are protected by a [Polly-style] circuit breaker written in C++20 coroutines.\n   409→\n   410→---\n   411→\n   412→## 6. High-Level Flow\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor Client as 🧑‍💻 Tenant / API Consumer\n    participant API as 🌐 Presentation & API\n    participant ORCH as 🔄 Orchestration\n    participant BUS as 📨 Event-Bus\n    participant SCN as 🛡️ ScannerSvc (Domain)\n    participant BCK as 💾 BackupSvc (Domain)\n    participant INF as 🛠️ Platform Adapter\n\n    Client->>API: POST /v1/scan\\n{asset: \"web-1\", tier:\"deep\"}\n    API->>ORCH: Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>\n    ORCH->>BUS: Publish(Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>)\n    %% Broadcast to interested domain services\n    BUS-->>SCN: Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>\n    SCN->>SCN: Validate + Enrich\n    SCN->>BUS: Emit(Evt<SecurityScanStarted>)\n    BUS-->>INF: Evt<SecurityScanStarted>\n    INF->>SCN: gRPC startScan()\n    INF-->>BUS: Evt<SecurityScanProgress>\n    BUS-->>SCN: Evt<SecurityScanProgress>\n    SCN->>BUS: Emit(Evt<SecurityScanCompleted>)\n    BUS-->>ORCH: Evt<SecurityScanCompleted>\n    ORCH->>Client: 200 OK {\"scanId\":\"...\",\"state\":\"COMPLETED\"}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Canonical Event Taxonomy\n\n| Layer              | Prefix | Example                     |\n|--------------------|--------|-----------------------------|\n| Command            | `Cmd`  | `Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>` |\n| Domain Event       | `Evt`  | `Evt<SecurityScanStarted>`  |\n| Integration Event  | `Int`  | `Int<InvoiceGenerated>`     |\n| Query / Response   | `Qry`  | `Qry<TenantUsage>`          |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Core C++ Building Blocks\n\nBelow snippets are **abridged** but compile-ready.  \nComplete sources reside in `src/eventing/`.\n\n### 3.1 Transport-Agnostic Envelope\n\n```cpp\n// eventing/Envelope.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <string>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>\n\nnamespace fl360::eventing {\n\nstruct Envelope\n{\n    std::string id;                 // UUIDv4\n    std::string type;               // Fully-qualified class name\n    std::chrono::system_clock::time_point timestamp;\n    nlohmann::json payload;         // Arbitrary JSON blob\n    std::string correlationId;      // For tracing call-chains\n\n    static Envelope wrap(const std::string& type,\n                         const nlohmann::json& payload,\n                         const std::string& correlation = {});\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::eventing\n```\n\n```cpp\n// eventing/Envelope.cpp\n#include \"Envelope.hpp\"\n#include <uuid.h>          // microsoft/GUID implementation\n#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n\nnamespace fl360::eventing {\n\nEnvelope Envelope::wrap(const std::string& type,\n                        const nlohmann::json& payload,\n                        const std::string& correlation)\n{\n    Envelope env;\n    env.id            = uuids::to_string(uuids::uuid_system_generator{}());\n    env.type          = type;\n    env.timestamp     = std::chrono::system_clock::now();\n    env.payload       = payload;\n    env.correlationId = correlation.empty() ? env.id : correlation;\n    return env;\n}\n\n} // namespace fl360::eventing\n```\n\n### 3.2 Command & Event Base Classes\n\n```cpp\n// eventing/Message.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <string_view>\n\nnamespace fl360::eventing {\n\nstruct Message {\n    virtual ~Message() = default;\n    virtual std::string_view name() const noexcept = 0;\n};\n\nstruct Command : public Message {};\nstruct DomainEvent : public Message {};\nstruct IntegrationEvent : public Message {};\n\n} // namespace fl360::eventing\n```\n\n### 3.3 Example Command Definition\n\n```cpp\n// api/commands/InitiateSecurityScan.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include \"eventing/Message.hpp\"\n#include <string>\n\nnamespace fl360::api::commands {\n\nstruct InitiateSecurityScan final : public fl360::eventing::Command\n{\n    std::string tenantId;\n    std::string assetId;\n    std::string scanTier;   // deep / quick / continuous\n\n    std::string_view name() const noexcept override\n    { return \"Cmd<InitiateSecurityScan>\"; }\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::api::commands\n```\n\n### 3.4 Chain-of-Responsibility Validation Pipeline\n\n```cpp\n// orchestration/ValidationChain.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <memory>\n#include \"eventing/Message.hpp\"\n\nnamespace fl360::orchestration {\n\nclass ValidationHandler\n{\npublic:\n    using Ptr = std::shared_ptr<ValidationHandler>;\n    virtual ~ValidationHandler() = default;\n\n    void linkWith(Ptr next) { next_ = std::move(next); }\n\n    bool validate(const fl360::eventing::Message& msg) const\n    {\n        if (!check(msg)) return false;\n        return !next_ || next_->validate(msg);\n    }\n\nprotected:\n    virtual bool check(const fl360::eventing::Message& msg) const = 0;\n\nprivate:\n    Ptr next_;\n};\n\nclass TenantQuotaValidator : public ValidationHandler\n{\nprotected:\n    bool check(const fl360::eventing::Message& msg) const override;\n};\n\nclass CompliancePolicyValidator : public ValidationHandler\n{\nprotected:\n    bool check(const fl360::eventing::Message& msg) const override;\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::orchestration\n```\n\n```cpp\n// orchestration/ValidationChain.cpp\n#include \"ValidationChain.hpp\"\n#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n\nnamespace fl360::orchestration {\n\nbool TenantQuotaValidator::check(const eventing::Message& msg) const\n{\n    spdlog::debug(\"TenantQuotaValidator: {}\", msg.name());\n    // TODO: Lookup tenant quota from Redis. Mocked here:\n    return true;\n}\n\nbool CompliancePolicyValidator::check(const eventing::Message& msg) const\n{\n    spdlog::debug(\"CompliancePolicyValidator: {}\", msg.name());\n    // TODO: Evaluate policies. Mocked:\n    return true;\n}\n\n} // namespace fl360::orchestration\n```\n\n### 3.5 In-Process Event Bus\n\n```cpp\n// eventing/InMemoryBus.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include <functional>\n#include <unordered_map>\n#include <vector>\n#include <shared_mutex>\n#include <typeindex>\n#include <any>\n\nnamespace fl360::eventing {\n\nclass InMemoryBus\n{\npublic:\n    template<typename TMsg>\n    using Handler = std::function<void(const TMsg&)>;\n\n    template<typename TMsg>\n    void publish(const TMsg& msg)\n    {\n        std::shared_lock lock(mutex_);\n        auto it = subscribers_.find(std::type_index(typeid(TMsg)));\n        if (it == subscribers_.end()) return;\n\n        for (auto& anyCb : it->second)\n        {\n            auto& cb = *std::any_cast<Handler<TMsg>>(&anyCb);\n            try { cb(msg); } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n                // Never kill the bus thread\n                spdlog::error(\"EventBus handler threw: {}\", ex.what());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    template<typename TMsg>\n    void subscribe(Handler<TMsg> handler)\n    {\n        std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);\n        subscribers_[std::type_index(typeid(TMsg))].push_back(handler);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::vector<std::any>> subscribers_;\n    mutable std::shared_mutex mutex_;\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::eventing\n```\n\n### 3.6 Domain Service Example – ScannerSvc\n\n```cpp\n// domain/scanner/ScannerService.hpp\n#pragma once\n#include \"eventing/InMemoryBus.hpp\"\n#include \"api/commands/InitiateSecurityScan.hpp\"\n#include <memory>\n\nnamespace fl360::domain::scanner {\n\nclass ScannerService\n{\npublic:\n    explicit ScannerService(std::shared_ptr<eventing::InMemoryBus> bus);\n\n    void start();\n\nprivate:\n    void handleScan(const api::commands::InitiateSecurityScan& cmd);\n\n    std::shared_ptr<eventing::InMemoryBus> bus_;\n};\n\n} // namespace fl360::domain::scanner\n```\n\n```cpp\n// domain/scanner/ScannerService.cpp\n#include \"ScannerService.hpp\"\n#include \"eventing/Envelope.hpp\"\n#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n\nnamespace fl360::domain::scanner {\n\nScannerService::ScannerService(std::shared_ptr<eventing::InMemoryBus> bus)\n    : bus_{std::move(bus)} {}\n\nvoid ScannerService::start()\n{\n    bus_->subscribe<api::commands::InitiateSecurityScan>(\n        [this](const auto& cmd) { handleScan(cmd); });\n}\n\nvoid ScannerService::handleScan(const api::commands::InitiateSecurityScan& cmd)\n{\n    spdlog::info(\"ScannerSvc received scan request for asset {}\", cmd.assetId);\n\n    // Emit a \"ScanStarted\" domain event\n    struct SecurityScanStarted : public eventing::DomainEvent\n    {\n        std::string scanId;\n        std::string_view name() const noexcept override\n        { return \"Evt<SecurityScanStarted>\"; }\n    } evt{.scanId = \"scan-\" + cmd.assetId};\n\n    bus_->publish(evt);\n\n    // Simulate long-running work (omitted)\n    // ...\n\n    struct SecurityScanCompleted : public eventing::DomainEvent\n    {\n        std::string scanId;\n        bool        findingsPresent{false};\n        std::string_view name() const noexcept override\n        { return \"Evt<SecurityScanCompleted>\"; }\n    } completed{.scanId = evt.scanId};\n\n    bus_->publish(completed);\n}\n\n} // namespace fl360::domain::scanner\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Putting It All Together\n\n```cpp\n// main.cpp\n#include \"eventing/InMemoryBus.hpp\"\n#include \"orchestration/ValidationChain.hpp\"\n#include \"domain/scanner/ScannerService.hpp\"\n#include \"api/commands/InitiateSecurityScan.hpp\"\n#include <spdlog/spdlog.h>\n\nint main()\n{\n    using namespace fl360;\n\n    auto bus = std::make_shared<eventing::InMemoryBus>();\n\n    // Bootstrap Scanner Domain Service\n    domain::scanner::ScannerService scanner{bus};\n    scanner.start();\n\n    // Build validation chain\n    auto quota   = std::make_shared<orchestration::TenantQuotaValidator>();\n    auto policy  = std::make_shared<orchestration::CompliancePolicyValidator>();\n    quota->linkWith(policy);\n\n    // Example command coming from API layer\n    api::commands::InitiateSecurityScan cmd{\n        .tenantId = \"tenant-42\",\n        .assetId  = \"web-front-1\",\n        .scanTier = \"deep\"\n    };\n\n    if (quota->validate(cmd))\n    {\n        spdlog::info(\"Validation passed – publishing command\");\n        bus->publish(cmd);\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        spdlog::warn(\"Validation failed – rejecting command\");\n    }\n\n    // Block until Ctrl-C (demo only)\n    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds{3});\n}\n```\n\n> The snippet demonstrates a minimal **local** topology.  \n> In production, the same abstractions map to **NATS JetStream** for publication, and gRPC-based **mesh sidecars** replace the in-process bus.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Failure Handling & Observability\n\n1. **Dead-Letter Queue** – When handler exceptions exceed a retry budget, the `Envelope` is routed to `DLQ.<ServiceName>` with exponential back-off metadata.  \n2. **Telemetry** – Each `Envelope` mutates the OpenTelemetry span context (via `correlationId`), enabling distributed tracing across gRPC boundaries.  \n3. **Circuit Breaker** – Outbound adapter calls (e.g., S3 backup storage) are protected by a [Polly-style] circuit breaker written in C++20 coroutines.\n\n---\n\n## 6. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012v7G55bfGJnmYnoB1mRP6Q","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# Architecture Decision Record: 002 — Event-Driven Architecture\n     3→\n     4→* **Status** : Accepted  \n     5→* **Date**   : 2024-06-08  \n     6→* **Stakeholders** :  \n     7→  * Product: VP Product, Lead Product Manager  \n     8→  * Engineering: Chief Architect, Platform Tech-Lead, Backend Guild  \n     9→  * Operations: SRE Lead, DevOps Lead  \n    10→  * Compliance & Security: CISO, Risk Officer  \n    11→\n    12→---\n    13→\n    14→## 1. Context  \n    15→\n    16→FortiLedger360 is expected to ingest and react to a high volume of fine-grained security events (e.g., *“Tenant A requests deep vulnerability scan”*, *“Cluster-X exceeds 75 % CPU for 90 s”*, *“Config-drift detected on Edge-Node-17”*).  \n    17→Traditional synchronous request/response workflows (REST or RPC) impose tight coupling, limited extensibility, and waterfall-style failure domains.  \n    18→\n    19→Key platform requirements:  \n    20→\n    21→* **Scalability** – Burst to > 250 K events/s while maintaining < 200 ms P95 processing latency.  \n    22→* **Extensibility** – Add a new service (e.g., `ComplianceAuditor`) with zero downtime.  \n    23→* **Fault Isolation** – Failure in `BackupNode` must **not** block `AlertBroker`.  \n    24→* **Observability** – Every state transition must emit traceable, structured audit logs.  \n    25→* **Multi-tenant Guardrails** – Tenants demand isolated QoS & configurable SLAs.  \n    26→\n    27→These constraints steer us toward **Event-Driven Architecture (EDA)** built around an **asynchronous, durable, high-throughput event bus**.\n    28→\n    29→---\n    30→\n    31→## 2. Decision  \n    32→\n    33→We will implement an Event-Driven Architecture in which:  \n    34→\n    35→1. **Domain events** are first-class citizens.  \n    36→2. **Producers** (e.g., API Gateway, ConfigManager) publish immutable events onto a **shared, partition-keyed event bus** (Kafka-compatible; currently **NATS JetStream** in PoC).  \n    37→3. **Consumers** (e.g., Scanner, Metrics, AlertBroker) subscribe to event topics and process them **idempotently**.  \n    38→4. **Commands** and **Queries** are explicitly separated (CQRS).  \n    39→5. **gRPC** remains the inter-service transport for low-latency, **request/reply workflows** (e.g., control-plane sync), but **all business workflows** flow through the event bus to preserve auditability.  \n    40→6. **Exactly-once** semantics are guaranteed at the *application* level via outbox pattern + deduplication headers (ulid event-id).  \n    41→7. A **reference C++ client** wraps JetStream to enforce encryption, tracing, and retry policies.\n    42→\n    43→---\n    44→\n    45→## 3. Consequences  \n    46→\n    47→### Positive  \n    48→* Loose coupling → Horizontal service agility.  \n    49→* Native scalability via topic sharding & consumer groups.  \n    50→* Natural audit log & replay capability → compliance win.  \n    51→* Enables decoupled failure handling and resiliency patterns (DLQ, back-pressure).  \n    52→\n    53→### Negative / Trade-offs  \n    54→* Added operational complexity: JetStream clusters, topic ACLs, and schema evolution.  \n    55→* Higher learning curve for developers unfamiliar with EDA patterns.  \n    56→* Latency overhead vs. sync RPC (≈ 5–15 ms per hop).  \n    57→\n    58→### Follow-ups  \n    59→* Implement **Schema Registry** (Apache Avro) + compatibility gates in CI.  \n    60→* Define **tenant partition-key strategy** for QoS isolation.  \n    61→* Provide **SDKs** (Go, Rust, C++) with consistent observability hooks.  \n    62→\n    63→---\n    64→\n    65→## 4. Reference Implementation (C++)\n    66→\n    67→Below is an excerpt from `messaging/EventBus.hpp` showcasing the thin, opinionated wrapper around NATS JetStream.  \n    68→It demonstrates publishing a `SecurityScanInitiated` event and consuming with **exactly-once** semantics.\n    69→\n    70→```cpp\n    71→#pragma once\n    72→#include <nats/nats.h>\n    73→#include <chrono>\n    74→#include <future>\n    75→#include <iostream>\n    76→#include <stdexcept>\n    77→#include <string>\n    78→#include <string_view>\n    79→#include <unordered_set>\n    80→\n    81→/**\n    82→ * @brief FortiLedger360 :: EventBus\n    83→ *\n    84→ * Opinionated, thread-safe JetStream wrapper providing:\n    85→ *   • Schema-validated payloads (JSON/Avro)\n    86→ *   • mTLS enforcement\n    87→ *   • Automatic ULID-based idempotency keys\n    88→ *   • Structured tracing (OpenTelemetry)\n    89→ */\n    90→class EventBus {\n    91→public:\n    92→    explicit EventBus(std::string_view natsUrl,\n    93→                      std::chrono::milliseconds timeout = std::chrono::seconds(5))\n    94→        : url_{natsUrl}, reqTimeout_{timeout}\n    95→    {\n    96→        natsOptions_Create(&opts_);\n    97→        natsOptions_SetURL(opts_, url_.c_str());\n    98→        // TODO: configure mTLS: natsOptions_SetSecureContext(...)\n    99→        auto s = natsConnection_ConnectTo(&conn_, opts_);\n   100→        if (s != NATS_OK) {\n   101→            throw std::runtime_error(\n   102→                \"EventBus: unable to connect to NATS: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n   103→        }\n   104→        // JetStream context\n   105→        s = natsConnection_JetStream(&js_, conn_, nullptr);\n   106→        if (s != NATS_OK) {\n   107→            throw std::runtime_error(\n   108→                \"EventBus: unable to create JetStream context: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n   109→        }\n   110→    }\n   111→\n   112→    ~EventBus()\n   113→    {\n   114→        if (js_)  natsJetStream_Destroy(js_);\n   115→        if (conn_) natsConnection_Destroy(conn_);\n   116→        if (opts_) natsOptions_Destroy(opts_);\n   117→    }\n   118→\n   119→    struct PublishAck {\n   120→        std::string stream;\n   121→        std::string domain;\n   122→        uint64_t    sequence{};\n   123→    };\n   124→\n   125→    // Publish JSONArray/Avro serialised bytes; async by default\n   126→    auto publish(std::string_view subject,\n   127→                 std::string_view payload,\n   128→                 std::string_view msgId /* ULID */,\n   129→                 bool synchronousAck = false) -> std::future<PublishAck>\n   130→    {\n   131→        // Headers: idempotency + trace\n   132→        natsMsg* msg{};\n   133→        natsMsg_Create(&msg, subject.data(), nullptr, payload.data(), payload.size());\n   134→        natsMsgHeader_Set(msg, \"Nats-Msg-Id\", msgId.data());\n   135→        // TODO: inject OpenTelemetry trace headers\n   136→\n   137→        return std::async(std::launch::async, [=] {\n   138→            jsPubAck* ack{};\n   139→            auto s =\n   140→                natsJetStream_PublishMsg(js_, &ack, msg,\n   141→                                         synchronousAck ? NATS_JETSTREAM_PUB_SYNCAWAIT : 0);\n   142→            natsMsg_Destroy(msg);\n   143→\n   144→            if (s != NATS_OK) {\n   145→                throw std::runtime_error(\"publish failed: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n   146→            }\n   147→\n   148→            PublishAck r{ack->Stream, ack->Domain, ack->Seq};\n   149→            jsPubAck_Destroy(ack);\n   150→            return r;\n   151→        });\n   152→    }\n   153→\n   154→    /**\n   155→     * Subscribe with at-least-once semantics.  \n   156→     * Application layer is responsible for idempotency using the message ULID.\n   157→     */\n   158→    void subscribe(std::string_view subject,\n   159→                   const std::function<void(const natsMsg*)>& handler,\n   160→                   std::string durableName = \"\")\n   161→    {\n   162→        natsSubscription* sub{};\n   163→        jsSubOpts opts{};\n   164→        jsSubOpts_Init(&opts);\n   165→        opts.Durable = durableName.empty() ? nullptr : durableName.c_str();\n   166→        opts.AckPolicy           = js_AckExplicit;\n   167→        opts.MaxDeliver          = 5; // retry/poison message threshold\n   168→        opts.IdleHeartbeat       = 30 * 1'000'000'000ULL; // 30s\n   169→\n   170→        auto s = natsJetStream_Subscribe(&sub, js_, subject.data(), nullptr,\n   171→                                         internalMsgCb, const_cast<void*>(static_cast<const void*>(&handler)), &opts);\n   172→        if (s != NATS_OK) {\n   173→            throw std::runtime_error(\"subscribe failed: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n   174→        }\n   175→        activeSubs_.emplace(sub);\n   176→    }\n   177→\n   178→private:\n   179→    static void internalMsgCb(natsConnection*, natsSubscription*, natsMsg* msg, void* closure)\n   180→    {\n   181→        auto* userCb = reinterpret_cast<std::function<void(const natsMsg*)>*>(closure);\n   182→        try {\n   183→            (*userCb)(msg); // userland processing\n   184→            natsMsg_Ack(msg); // explicit ack\n   185→        } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n   186→            std::cerr << \"[EventBus] handler exception: \" << ex.what() << '\\n';\n   187→            natsMsg_Nak(msg, nullptr, 0); // negative ack → redelivery\n   188→        }\n   189→        natsMsg_Destroy(msg);\n   190→    }\n   191→\n   192→    std::string                     url_;\n   193→    std::chrono::milliseconds       reqTimeout_{};\n   194→    natsOptions*                    opts_{};\n   195→    natsConnection*                 conn_{};\n   196→    natsJetStreamContext*           js_{};\n   197→    std::unordered_set<natsSubscription*> activeSubs_;\n   198→};\n   199→```\n   200→\n   201→Usage sample for the `Scanner` service:\n   202→\n   203→```cpp\n   204→#include \"messaging/EventBus.hpp\"\n   205→#include <ulid/ulid.hpp> // hypothetical ULID lib\n   206→\n   207→int main() {\n   208→    EventBus bus{\"tls://nats.internal.fortiledger360.io:4222\"};\n   209→\n   210→    // Producer side: announce that a scan has started.\n   211→    auto ulid = ulid::generate();\n   212→    bus.publish(\n   213→        \"security.scan.initiated\",\n   214→        R\"({\"tenant\":\"acme\",\"cluster\":\"edge-a1\",\"depth\":\"deep\"})\",\n   215→        ulid)\n   216→        .get(); // wait for ack\n   217→\n   218→    // Consumer side: listen for completion\n   219→    bus.subscribe(\"security.scan.completed\", [](const natsMsg* msg) {\n   220→        std::string payload{natsMsg_GetData(msg), natsMsg_GetDataLength(msg)};\n   221→        // TODO: parse JSON & persist results\n   222→        std::cout << \"[Scanner] Received scan.completed: \" << payload << '\\n';\n   223→    }, \"scanner-completed-durable\");\n   224→\n   225→    // Block main thread (demo purposes)\n   226→    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::hours(24));\n   227→}\n   228→```\n   229→\n   230→---\n   231→\n   232→## 5. Context  \n\nFortiLedger360 is expected to ingest and react to a high volume of fine-grained security events (e.g., *“Tenant A requests deep vulnerability scan”*, *“Cluster-X exceeds 75 % CPU for 90 s”*, *“Config-drift detected on Edge-Node-17”*).  \nTraditional synchronous request/response workflows (REST or RPC) impose tight coupling, limited extensibility, and waterfall-style failure domains.  \n\nKey platform requirements:  \n\n* **Scalability** – Burst to > 250 K events/s while maintaining < 200 ms P95 processing latency.  \n* **Extensibility** – Add a new service (e.g., `ComplianceAuditor`) with zero downtime.  \n* **Fault Isolation** – Failure in `BackupNode` must **not** block `AlertBroker`.  \n* **Observability** – Every state transition must emit traceable, structured audit logs.  \n* **Multi-tenant Guardrails** – Tenants demand isolated QoS & configurable SLAs.  \n\nThese constraints steer us toward **Event-Driven Architecture (EDA)** built around an **asynchronous, durable, high-throughput event bus**.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Decision  \n\nWe will implement an Event-Driven Architecture in which:  \n\n1. **Domain events** are first-class citizens.  \n2. **Producers** (e.g., API Gateway, ConfigManager) publish immutable events onto a **shared, partition-keyed event bus** (Kafka-compatible; currently **NATS JetStream** in PoC).  \n3. **Consumers** (e.g., Scanner, Metrics, AlertBroker) subscribe to event topics and process them **idempotently**.  \n4. **Commands** and **Queries** are explicitly separated (CQRS).  \n5. **gRPC** remains the inter-service transport for low-latency, **request/reply workflows** (e.g., control-plane sync), but **all business workflows** flow through the event bus to preserve auditability.  \n6. **Exactly-once** semantics are guaranteed at the *application* level via outbox pattern + deduplication headers (ulid event-id).  \n7. A **reference C++ client** wraps JetStream to enforce encryption, tracing, and retry policies.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Consequences  \n\n### Positive  \n* Loose coupling → Horizontal service agility.  \n* Native scalability via topic sharding & consumer groups.  \n* Natural audit log & replay capability → compliance win.  \n* Enables decoupled failure handling and resiliency patterns (DLQ, back-pressure).  \n\n### Negative / Trade-offs  \n* Added operational complexity: JetStream clusters, topic ACLs, and schema evolution.  \n* Higher learning curve for developers unfamiliar with EDA patterns.  \n* Latency overhead vs. sync RPC (≈ 5–15 ms per hop).  \n\n### Follow-ups  \n* Implement **Schema Registry** (Apache Avro) + compatibility gates in CI.  \n* Define **tenant partition-key strategy** for QoS isolation.  \n* Provide **SDKs** (Go, Rust, C++) with consistent observability hooks.  \n\n---\n\n## 4. Reference Implementation (C++)\n\nBelow is an excerpt from `messaging/EventBus.hpp` showcasing the thin, opinionated wrapper around NATS JetStream.  \nIt demonstrates publishing a `SecurityScanInitiated` event and consuming with **exactly-once** semantics.\n\n```cpp\n#pragma once\n#include <nats/nats.h>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <future>\n#include <iostream>\n#include <stdexcept>\n#include <string>\n#include <string_view>\n#include <unordered_set>\n\n/**\n * @brief FortiLedger360 :: EventBus\n *\n * Opinionated, thread-safe JetStream wrapper providing:\n *   • Schema-validated payloads (JSON/Avro)\n *   • mTLS enforcement\n *   • Automatic ULID-based idempotency keys\n *   • Structured tracing (OpenTelemetry)\n */\nclass EventBus {\npublic:\n    explicit EventBus(std::string_view natsUrl,\n                      std::chrono::milliseconds timeout = std::chrono::seconds(5))\n        : url_{natsUrl}, reqTimeout_{timeout}\n    {\n        natsOptions_Create(&opts_);\n        natsOptions_SetURL(opts_, url_.c_str());\n        // TODO: configure mTLS: natsOptions_SetSecureContext(...)\n        auto s = natsConnection_ConnectTo(&conn_, opts_);\n        if (s != NATS_OK) {\n            throw std::runtime_error(\n                \"EventBus: unable to connect to NATS: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n        }\n        // JetStream context\n        s = natsConnection_JetStream(&js_, conn_, nullptr);\n        if (s != NATS_OK) {\n            throw std::runtime_error(\n                \"EventBus: unable to create JetStream context: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    ~EventBus()\n    {\n        if (js_)  natsJetStream_Destroy(js_);\n        if (conn_) natsConnection_Destroy(conn_);\n        if (opts_) natsOptions_Destroy(opts_);\n    }\n\n    struct PublishAck {\n        std::string stream;\n        std::string domain;\n        uint64_t    sequence{};\n    };\n\n    // Publish JSONArray/Avro serialised bytes; async by default\n    auto publish(std::string_view subject,\n                 std::string_view payload,\n                 std::string_view msgId /* ULID */,\n                 bool synchronousAck = false) -> std::future<PublishAck>\n    {\n        // Headers: idempotency + trace\n        natsMsg* msg{};\n        natsMsg_Create(&msg, subject.data(), nullptr, payload.data(), payload.size());\n        natsMsgHeader_Set(msg, \"Nats-Msg-Id\", msgId.data());\n        // TODO: inject OpenTelemetry trace headers\n\n        return std::async(std::launch::async, [=] {\n            jsPubAck* ack{};\n            auto s =\n                natsJetStream_PublishMsg(js_, &ack, msg,\n                                         synchronousAck ? NATS_JETSTREAM_PUB_SYNCAWAIT : 0);\n            natsMsg_Destroy(msg);\n\n            if (s != NATS_OK) {\n                throw std::runtime_error(\"publish failed: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n            }\n\n            PublishAck r{ack->Stream, ack->Domain, ack->Seq};\n            jsPubAck_Destroy(ack);\n            return r;\n        });\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Subscribe with at-least-once semantics.  \n     * Application layer is responsible for idempotency using the message ULID.\n     */\n    void subscribe(std::string_view subject,\n                   const std::function<void(const natsMsg*)>& handler,\n                   std::string durableName = \"\")\n    {\n        natsSubscription* sub{};\n        jsSubOpts opts{};\n        jsSubOpts_Init(&opts);\n        opts.Durable = durableName.empty() ? nullptr : durableName.c_str();\n        opts.AckPolicy           = js_AckExplicit;\n        opts.MaxDeliver          = 5; // retry/poison message threshold\n        opts.IdleHeartbeat       = 30 * 1'000'000'000ULL; // 30s\n\n        auto s = natsJetStream_Subscribe(&sub, js_, subject.data(), nullptr,\n                                         internalMsgCb, const_cast<void*>(static_cast<const void*>(&handler)), &opts);\n        if (s != NATS_OK) {\n            throw std::runtime_error(\"subscribe failed: \" + std::string(natsStatus_GetText(s)));\n        }\n        activeSubs_.emplace(sub);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    static void internalMsgCb(natsConnection*, natsSubscription*, natsMsg* msg, void* closure)\n    {\n        auto* userCb = reinterpret_cast<std::function<void(const natsMsg*)>*>(closure);\n        try {\n            (*userCb)(msg); // userland processing\n            natsMsg_Ack(msg); // explicit ack\n        } catch (const std::exception& ex) {\n            std::cerr << \"[EventBus] handler exception: \" << ex.what() << '\\n';\n            natsMsg_Nak(msg, nullptr, 0); // negative ack → redelivery\n        }\n        natsMsg_Destroy(msg);\n    }\n\n    std::string                     url_;\n    std::chrono::milliseconds       reqTimeout_{};\n    natsOptions*                    opts_{};\n    natsConnection*                 conn_{};\n    natsJetStreamContext*           js_{};\n    std::unordered_set<natsSubscription*> activeSubs_;\n};\n```\n\nUsage sample for the `Scanner` service:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"messaging/EventBus.hpp\"\n#include <ulid/ulid.hpp> // hypothetical ULID lib\n\nint main() {\n    EventBus bus{\"tls://nats.internal.fortiledger360.io:4222\"};\n\n    // Producer side: announce that a scan has started.\n    auto ulid = ulid::generate();\n    bus.publish(\n        \"security.scan.initiated\",\n        R\"({\"tenant\":\"acme\",\"cluster\":\"edge-a1\",\"depth\":\"deep\"})\",\n        ulid)\n        .get(); // wait for ack\n\n    // Consumer side: listen for completion\n    bus.subscribe(\"security.scan.completed\", [](const natsMsg* msg) {\n        std::string payload{natsMsg_GetData(msg), natsMsg_GetDataLength(msg)};\n        // TODO: parse JSON & persist results\n        std::cout << \"[Scanner] Received scan.completed: \" << payload << '\\n';\n    }, \"scanner-completed-durable\");\n\n    // Block main thread (demo purposes)\n    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::hours(24));\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Context  \n    13→FortiLedger360 consists of ~40 micro-services distributed across five bounded-context\n    14→layers.  The initial prototype relied on *REST/JSON* for inter-service communication and\n    15→used a bespoke DNS+Consul mechanism for discovery.  As we approach General Availability\n    16→and a target throughput of 35 K cross-service calls per second, the following pain-points\n    17→have become critical:\n    18→\n    19→* High per-call latency caused by HTTP/1.1 + JSON (≈ 1.7 ms serialisation cost alone).  \n    20→* No first-class streaming semantics — mandatory for continuous vulnerability scans.  \n    21→* Ad-hoc security: hand-rolled JWTs, static API keys, no mutual-TLS.  \n    22→* Operational blind-spots: we cannot uniformly enforce retries, circuit-breakers, or\n    23→  trace context propagation.  \n    24→\n    25→### 2. Decision  \n    26→We will:\n    27→\n    28→1. Adopt **gRPC (HTTP/2)** as the canonical RPC protocol between internal services.  \n    29→2. Mandate **mutual-TLS** using the platform’s Certificate-Authority for every gRPC call.  \n    30→3. Deploy all workloads **behind Envoy sidecars** (managed by Istio¹) to realise a\n    31→   zero-trust **Service Mesh** featuring:  \n    32→   * mTLS termination & rotation  \n    33→   * out-of-the-box retries, circuit-breaking, deadline and back-off policies  \n    34→   * distributed tracing via OpenTelemetry → Jaeger  \n    35→   * L7 metrics and rate-limiting  \n    36→4. Generate C++ stubs via `protoc --cpp_out` for low-latency services written in C++17\n    37→   (Scanner, Metrics, BackupNode).  Java/Kotlin, Go and Rust services will continue to\n    38→   use language-appropriate gRPC bindings.  \n    39→\n    40→¹ Istio can be swapped with Consul Connect or Kuma if future requirements dictate.\n    41→\n    42→### 3. Consequences  \n    43→\n    44→* (+) 4–6× faster intra-cluster calls (binary Protobuf, header compression, multiplexed\n    45→  streams).  \n    46→* (+) Uniform security posture: every hop is authenticated & encrypted.  \n    47→* (+) Mesh provides traffic-shaping without code changes.  \n    48→* (±) Incremental learning curve: teams must master Protobuf, gRPC error-model, Envoy.  \n    49→* (–) Additional sidecar footprint (~80 MiB RAM / pod) and control-plane overhead.  \n    50→\n    51→---\n    52→\n    53→## 4. Reference Implementation (C++ 17)\n    54→\n    55→Below is a distilled yet functional excerpt taken from the *Scanner* bounded context.  \n    56→The code compiles with **grpc 1.61+**, **OpenSSL 3** You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f14367f7-503f-4acf-8fd0-3a7f9ede3bf3","uuid":"ddeb3a7a-6717-4480-b99b-77757b3a0923","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/project/docs/architecture/adr/003-grpc-and-service-mesh.md","content":"```markdown\n# Architecture Decision Record 003  \n## Title: Standardise on gRPC over mTLS inside an Envoy-based Service Mesh\n\n* Status: **Accepted**  \n* Date: 2024-06-11  \n* Supersedes / Amends: None  \n* Authors: Platform & Runtime Chapter  \n\n---\n\n### 1. Context  \nFortiLedger360 consists of ~40 micro-services distributed across five bounded-context\nlayers.  The initial prototype relied on *REST/JSON* for inter-service communication and\nused a bespoke DNS+Consul mechanism for discovery.  As we approach General Availability\nand a target throughput of 35 K cross-service calls per second, the following pain-points\nhave become critical:\n\n* High per-call latency caused by HTTP/1.1 + JSON (≈ 1.7 ms serialisation cost alone).  \n* No first-class streaming semantics — mandatory for continuous vulnerability scans.  \n* Ad-hoc security: hand-rolled JWTs, static API keys, no mutual-TLS.  \n* Operational blind-spots: we cannot uniformly enforce retries, circuit-breakers, or\n  trace context propagation.  \n\n### 2. Decision  \nWe will:\n\n1. Adopt **gRPC (HTTP/2)** as the canonical RPC protocol between internal services.  \n2. Mandate **mutual-TLS** using the platform’s Certificate-Authority for every gRPC call.  \n3. Deploy all workloads **behind Envoy sidecars** (managed by Istio¹) to realise a\n   zero-trust **Service Mesh** featuring:  \n   * mTLS termination & rotation  \n   * out-of-the-box retries, circuit-breaking, deadline and back-off policies  \n   * distributed tracing via OpenTelemetry → Jaeger  \n   * L7 metrics and rate-limiting  \n4. Generate C++ stubs via `protoc --cpp_out` for low-latency services written in C++17\n   (Scanner, Metrics, BackupNode).  Java/Kotlin, Go and Rust services will continue to\n   use language-appropriate gRPC bindings.  \n\n¹ Istio can be swapped with Consul Connect or Kuma if future requirements dictate.\n\n### 3. Consequences  \n\n* (+) 4–6× faster intra-cluster calls (binary Protobuf, header compression, multiplexed\n  streams).  \n* (+) Uniform security posture: every hop is authenticated & encrypted.  \n* (+) Mesh provides traffic-shaping without code changes.  \n* (±) Incremental learning curve: teams must master Protobuf, gRPC error-model, Envoy.  \n* (–) Additional sidecar footprint (~80 MiB RAM / pod) and control-plane overhead.  \n\n---\n\n## 4. Reference Implementation (C++ 17)\n\nBelow is a distilled yet functional excerpt taken from the *Scanner* bounded context.  \nThe code compiles with **grpc 1.61+**, **OpenSSL 3** and enables *sanitizers* in `CMakeLists.txt`.\n\n### 4.1 `scanner.proto`\n\n```proto\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\n\npackage fortiledger360.scanner.v1;\n\noption java_package = \"com.fortiledger360.scanner.v1\";\noption go_package   = \"fortiledger360/scanner/v1;scannerpb\";\n\n// Command Pattern — invokes an asynchronous scan.\nmessage InitiateSecurityScanRequest {\n  string tenant_id   = 1;\n  string resource_id = 2; // VM, container, or k8s-namespace\n  enum Depth {\n    DEPTH_UNSPECIFIED = 0;\n    QUICK             = 1;\n    DEEP              = 2;\n  }\n  Depth depth = 3;\n}\n\nmessage InitiateSecurityScanResponse {\n  string scan_id = 1;\n}\n\n// Observer/Strategy Pattern — stream incremental findings.\nmessage ScanFinding {\n  string vulnerability_id = 1;\n  string severity         = 2;\n  string description      = 3;\n  int64  detected_at_ms   = 4;\n}\n\nservice SecurityScanner {\n  rpc InitiateSecurityScan (InitiateSecurityScanRequest)\n        returns (InitiateSecurityScanResponse);\n\n  // Server-side streaming for real-time dashboards.\n  rpc StreamFindings (google.protobuf.StringValue) // scan_id\n        returns (stream ScanFinding);\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 `security_scanner_server.cpp`\n\n```cpp\n/**\n * @file security_scanner_server.cpp\n * @brief gRPC server for the SecurityScanner bounded context.\n *\n * Build:\n *   $ cmake -B build -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=RelWithDebInfo\n *   $ cmake --build build -j\n */\n\n#include <csignal>\n#include <filesystem>\n#include <fstream>\n#include <grpcpp/ext/proto_server_reflection_plugin.h>\n#include <grpcpp/grpcpp.h>\n\n#include \"scanner.grpc.pb.h\"\n\nusing fortiledger360::scanner::v1::SecurityScanner;\nusing fortiledger360::scanner::v1::InitiateSecurityScanRequest;\nusing fortiledger360::scanner::v1::InitiateSecurityScanResponse;\nusing fortiledger360::scanner::v1::ScanFinding;\n\nnamespace fs = std::filesystem;\n\nstatic std::atomic_bool g_terminate{false};\n\nclass SecurityScannerServiceImpl final : public SecurityScanner::Service\n{\npublic:\n    grpc::Status InitiateSecurityScan(grpc::ServerContext* context,\n                                      const InitiateSecurityScanRequest* request,\n                                      InitiateSecurityScanResponse* response) override\n    {\n        if (request->tenant_id().empty() || request->resource_id().empty())\n        {\n            return grpc::Status(grpc::StatusCode::INVALID_ARGUMENT,\n                                \"tenant_id and resource_id must be set\");\n        }\n\n        // Naïve UUID — replace with a robust generator in prod.\n        const std::string scan_id = \"scan-\" + std::to_string(++scan_counter_);\n        response->set_scan_id(scan_id);\n\n        // Off-load heavy scan to a dedicated thread-pool.\n        std::thread{&SecurityScannerServiceImpl::performScan, this,\n                    scan_id, *request}.detach();\n\n        return grpc::Status::OK;\n    }\n\n    grpc::Status StreamFindings(grpc::ServerContext* context,\n                                const google::protobuf::StringValue* scan_id_val,\n                                grpc::ServerWriter<ScanFinding>* writer) override\n    {\n        const std::string scan_id = scan_id_val->value();\n        absl::ReaderMutexLock lk(&findings_mu_);\n        auto it = findings_.find(scan_id);\n        if (it == findings_.end())\n        {\n            return grpc::Status(grpc::StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, \"Unknown scan_id\");\n        }\n\n        for (const auto& finding : it->second)\n        {\n            if (!writer->Write(finding))\n            {\n                // Client aborted.\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        return grpc::Status::OK;\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void performScan(std::string scan_id, InitiateSecurityScanRequest request)\n    {\n        // Simulate scan workflow. In reality this would call ClamAV, Trivy, etc.\n        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(250));\n\n        ScanFinding f;\n        f.set_vulnerability_id(\"CVE-2023-1234\");\n        f.set_severity(\"HIGH\");\n        f.set_description(\"Sample buffer-overflow vuln\");\n        f.set_detected_at_ms(\n            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n                std::chrono::system_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count());\n\n        {\n            absl::WriterMutexLock lk(&findings_mu_);\n            findings_[scan_id].push_back(std::move(f));\n        }\n\n        // Persist to event-bus for further processing (omitted).\n    }\n\n    std::atomic_uint64_t scan_counter_{0};\n    absl::Mutex findings_mu_;\n    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<ScanFinding>> findings_ ABSL_GUARDED_BY(findings_mu_);\n};\n\nstatic void handleSignal(int)\n{\n    g_terminate.store(true);\n}\n\nstatic std::string readFile(const fs::path& p)\n{\n    std::ifstream ifs(p, std::ios::binary);\n    if (!ifs)\n        throw std::runtime_error(\"Unable to open \" + p.string());\n    return {std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(ifs), {}};\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char* argv[])\n{\n    const fs::path kCertDir   = \"/etc/fortiledger360/certs/\";\n    const fs::path kCertFile  = kCertDir / \"server.crt\";\n    const fs::path kKeyFile   = kCertDir / \"server.key\";\n    const fs::path kCaFile    = kCertDir / \"ca.crt\";\n\n    grpc::SslServerCredentialsOptions::PemKeyCertPair pkcp{\n        readFile(kKeyFile), readFile(kCertFile)};\n    grpc::SslServerCredentialsOptions ssl_opts;\n    ssl_opts.pem_key_cert_pairs.push_back(pkcp);\n    ssl_opts.pem_root_certs = readFile(kCaFile);\n    ssl_opts.client_certificate_request =\n        GRPC_SSL_REQUEST_AND_REQUIRE_CLIENT_CERTIFICATE_AND_VERIFY;\n\n    std::shared_ptr<grpc::ServerCredentials> creds =\n        grpc::SslServerCredentials(ssl_opts);\n\n    SecurityScannerServiceImpl service;\n\n    grpc::EnableDefaultHealthCheckService(true);\n    grpc::reflection::InitProtoReflectionServerBuilderPlugin();\n\n    grpc::ServerBuilder builder;\n    builder.AddListeningPort(\"0.0.0.0:50051\", creds);\n    builder.RegisterService(&service);\n    std::unique_ptr<grpc::Server> server(builder.BuildAndStart());\n\n    std::signal(SIGINT, handleSignal);\n    std::signal(SIGTERM, handleSignal);\n\n    std::cout << \"SecurityScanner gRPC server started on :50051 (mTLS enabled)\\n\";\n    while (!g_terminate.load())\n        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds(1));\n\n    server->Shutdown();\n    server->Wait();\n    std::cout << \"SecurityScanner gracefully terminated.\\n\";\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 `security_scanner_client.cpp`\n\n```cpp\n/**\n * @file security_scanner_client.cpp\n * @brief Example envoy-injected client that calls the scanner via mTLS.\n */\n\n#include <grpcpp/grpcpp.h>\n#include \"scanner.grpc.pb.h\"\n\nusing fortiledger360::scanner::v1::SecurityScanner;\nusing fortiledger360::scanner::v1::InitiateSecurityScanRequest;\nusing fortiledger360::scanner::v1::InitiateSecurityScanResponse;\n\nstatic std::shared_ptr<grpc::Channel> makeSecureChannel()\n{\n    grpc::SslCredentialsOptions ssl_opts;\n    ssl_opts.pem_root_certs = \"\"; // Envoy sidecar performs TLS => plaintext loopback\n    return grpc::CreateChannel(\"127.0.0.1:15001\", grpc::InsecureChannelCredentials());\n}\n\nint main()\n{\n    auto channel = makeSecureChannel();\n    std::unique_ptr<SecurityScanner::Stub> stub = SecurityScanner::NewStub(channel);\n\n    InitiateSecurityScanRequest req;\n    req.set_tenant_id(\"tenant-42\");\n    req.set_resource_id(\"k8s-namespace-prod\");\n    req.set_depth(InitiateSecurityScanRequest::DEPTH::InitiateSecurityScanRequest_Depth_DEEP);\n\n    grpc::ClientContext ctx;\n    InitiateSecurityScanResponse resp;\n    grpc::Status status = stub->InitiateSecurityScan(&ctx, req, &resp);\n\n    if (!status.ok())\n    {\n        std::cerr << \"RPC failed: \" << status.error_message() << '\\n';\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n    std::cout << \"Scan ID: \" << resp.scan_id() << '\\n';\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Operational Topology\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    subgraph Service Mesh (Istio)\n        SC[SecurityScanner] <-- gRPC/mTLS --> MN[MetricsNode]\n        SC <-- gRPC/mTLS --> BN[BackupNode]\n        SC <-- gRPC/mTLS --> AB[AlertBroker]\n    end\n    classDef svc fill:#0f5e9c,stroke:#fff,stroke-width:2px,color:#fff;\n    class SC,MN,BN,AB svc;\n```\n\nEnvoy sidecars transparently enforce per-method time-outs, automatic retries (`retryOn:\n5xx,connect-failure,refused-stream`) and distributed traces exported to the\nOpenTelemetry Collector.\n\n---\n\n### 6. Risk Mitigation & Future Work  \n\n* Blue-green roll-outs validated in a staging mesh before prod promotion.  \n* Pen-tests scheduled to validate certificate rotation and downgrade attack resilience.  \n* Evaluate HTTP/3 + gQUIC once officially supported by gRPC-C++.  \n\n---\n\n### 7. Rejected Alternatives\n\n| Alternative | Rationale for Rejection |\n|-------------|-------------------------|\n| Continue with REST/JSON + Custom TLS | Latency & missing streaming semantics outweighed familiarity benefits. |\n| Apache Thrift | We already depend on gRPC in the Java pipeline; doubling infra was unjustified. |\n| ZeroMQ | Lack of built-in authN/Z, difficult to operate at scale in Kubernetes. |\n\n---\n\nThis ADR is **accepted** and effective immediately.  All new internal service\ninterfaces **must** be defined in Protobuf and exposed via gRPC; legacy endpoints\nare slated for migration over the next two quarters.\n```","numLines":348,"startLine":1,"totalLines":348}}}

for (const auto& mw : middleware_pipeline_)\n    {\n        bool proceed = false;\n        try\n        {\n            proceed = mw->Handle(ctx);\n        }\n        catch (const std::exception& ex)\n        {\n            FL360_LOG_ERROR(\"Middleware [{}] threw exception: {}\", typeid(*mw).name(), ex.what());\n            return { CommandStatus::Failed, ex.what() };\n        }\n\n        if (!proceed)\n        {\n            return { CommandStatus::Failed, \"Middleware aborted execution.\" };\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2) Locate executor\n    std::shared_ptr<IExecutorWrapper> executor;\n    {\n        std::lock_guard lock(m_);\n        const std::type_index key(typeid(*command));\n        const auto            it = executors_.find(key);\n        if (it == executors_.end())\n        {\n            return { CommandStatus::Failed,\n                     \"No executor registered for command type: \" + std::string(key.name()) };\n        }\n        executor = it->second;\n    }\n\n    // 3) Execute\n    try\n    {\n        return executor->Execute(command, ctx);\n    }\n    catch (const std::exception& ex)\n    {\n        FL360_LOG_ERROR(\"Executor for [{}] threw exception: {}\", command->name(), ex.what());\n        return { CommandStatus::Failed, ex.what() };\n    }\n}\n\ninline std::future<CommandResult> CommandHandler::DispatchAsync(const std::shared_ptr<ICommand>& command)\n{\n    // The captured shared_ptr (self) guarantees the handler's lifetime for the async task.\n    auto self = shared_from_this();\n    return std::async(std::launch::async, [self, command] { return self->Dispatch(command); });\n}\n\n} // namespace fortiledger::orchestration\n```","numLines":346,"startLine":1,"totalLines":346}}}
